## 90. Antigravity (Gemini) subagents show in the subagent panel

- **Get:** a Gemini (Antigravity) agent that starts subagents (`invoke_subagent`, `start_subagent`) shows them as Claude's do: the "Running N subagents" card in the chat, the "N working" dock and the "Subagent" side panel. Each card has the subagent's `Role` as its title, `self` as its type, its task, its model, and its own steps (commands, reads, text, thinking). It is "running" from the call, "completed" with its report when it reports to the agent (`send_message`), "stopped" when the agent kills it (`manage_subagents` kill) or its process ends, and "failed" with the reason when OpenBot found no session for it. Before, Gemini chats showed no subagent at all.
- **Why:** Elias, 22 Nov 2026 (screenshot 19:44): Vegapunk said "All 5 background subagents are running in parallel" and the chat had no subagent card or tab, while Claude chats have one.
- **Root cause:** the panel reads `turn/subagents/updated`, which the ACP client makes only from `AcpFlavor.subagentTools` calls (Kimi, Cline, Hermes; `acp-client.ts:4467` on `main`) or OpenCode's child updates (`:4481`). The Antigravity flavor had neither (`acp-flavor-antigravity.ts:635`), and the updates of the subagent sessions that #84 resumes were dropped (`acp-client.ts:3548`). Antigravity 1.5.14 names a subagent over ACP nowhere: `invoke_subagent` completes at once with no output, and only the resumed child session (#84) sends its steps and its `send_message` report (lab capture `RE-antigravity-subagent-perms/captures/C-resume-child/acp.ndjson`).
- **Vegapunk's 5 subagents were real:** `brain/be684917…` (TL), `b6a3f404…` (TR), `669d79bb…` (BL), `e3b5c3ad…` (BR), `477a1507…` (blind SFX), each with a first message from Vegapunk's session `6e9cd6d0…` (`invoke_subagent`, `Role` "Khizar TL Quadrant Harvester" …), started 19:41:57–19:42:54. All 5 ended at 19:43:08: OpenBot closed Vegapunk's session (`Got signal terminated`, ACP log `acp-server-20261122_191005-74187.log:1738`; Antigravity runs a process per session, `sessionProcesses`), 2.8 s after the turn ended and 8 ms after a `servers:mcp:set-enabled` (trace). The MCP refresh waits only for a client's `holdsBackgroundWork`, which the ACP client does not have (`thread-lifecycle.ts:1864`). So at 19:44 they no longer ran. Not fixed here (it would change idle release for every ACP agent); with this entry their cards would have shown "stopped" at 19:43:08.
- **How (portable):** `AcpFlavor.subagentSessions` gets `launches` (cards from the call's arguments), `report` (a call that reports to a session) and `stops` (sessions a call kills); `children` also returns each child's task. `acp-subagent-updates.ts`: `foldSessionSubagentCall` makes one background card per subagent in the parent's turn (the turn must still run, or `turn-lifecycle` keeps no card); `linkSessionSubagent` ties a found child session to the card with the same task (else the call's first free card; a nested child shows in its top card); `foldSessionSubagentUpdate` turns the child's tool calls and chunks into steps, and a `send_message` to the parent's session into the report and "completed"; `failUnlinkedSessionSubagents` ends a card whose session was not found in 15 s; `stopSessionSubagents` for kills. `acp-client.ts`: the child's updates refresh the parent's turn and work hold. Antigravity: `Role`/`Prompt`/`TypeName`/`Model` (`inherit` = the parent's), `send_message {Recipient, Message}`, `manage_subagents {Action: "kill", ConversationIds}`. Read-only on Antigravity's folder.
- **Here:** `src/backend/acp-flavor.ts`, `src/backend/acp-flavor-antigravity.ts`, `src/backend/agent/acp-subagent-updates.ts`, `src/backend/acp-client.ts`, key `error.provider.subagentSessionNotFound` (15 languages), `scripts/antigravity-subagent-permissions-e2e.ts`, `scripts/provider-fakes/agents/antigravity-subagent.ts`, stories `Conversation/Subagents` › `GeminiRunning`, `GeminiEnded`, `GeminiPanel`, `GeminiDock`.
- **Tests:** `bun run test:antigravity-subagent-permissions` (fake `agy acp`): the #84 cases plus the panel: a card "running" at the call, then the child's steps and "completed" with the report (accepted and declined), two `start_subagent` children each in its own card (folders sorted against the list), a killed one "stopped", one with no session "failed" (this replaces the old 2 s sleep). PASS; with `main`'s `src/backend` and `packages/i18n` it FAILS with no `turn/subagents/updated` at all. `test:acp-subagents`, `antigravity-steer`, `antigravity-tool-filter`, `antigravity-failing-session`: PASS. `typecheck:node`, `typecheck:renderer`, `typecheck:storybook`, `i18n:check` clean; Biome clean on changed files. Story shots in headless Chrome: `~/OpenBot/Shared/gotham/ag-subagent-panel/`.
- **Not covered:** the live app was not rebuilt or run against the real CLI with this change (the e2e uses a fake that copies the probe's ACP traffic). A subagent that never calls `send_message` stays "running" until it is killed, its parent's session closes, or its process ends (Antigravity sends no other end). `start_subagent`'s list fields are read like `invoke_subagent`'s; no live `start_subagent` call was seen. The session close on an MCP change (above) is a separate fix.
- **Check:** ask a Gemini agent: "start a self subagent that runs `pwd && echo shell-ok` and reports it". The chat shows "Running 1 subagent", the row opens the Subagent panel with the command step, and the row turns done with `shell-ok`.

## 88. Agents can use your real Chrome profile (opt-in, off by default)

- **Get:** Settings › Computer Use › "Your Chrome" › **Let agents use my Chrome profile** (off by default). On, after the next OpenBot restart, an agent that needs your sign-ins, cookies or open tabs attaches Computer Use to your running Chrome window (`browser_prepare`, strategy `existing_profile`) instead of `openbot_browser`. No OpenBot card or agent question before the attach: the switch is the approval, and the agent may use `delivery_mode: "foreground"` when the driver refuses with `background_unavailable`. Chrome's own "Allow remote debugging?" prompt stays yours: agents never click it and ask you to click Allow. A save shows "✓ Saved"; while the running driver has the other choice, the row says "Restart OpenBot to apply this change." with a Restart OpenBot button (the idle restart of Settings › Updates).
- **Why:** Elias, 22 Nov 2026: "Add a setting", off by default; "I don't want to have to approve it every time when it's on."
- **How (portable):**
  - Driver: `cua-driver serve --socket <path> --no-overlay` + `--grant existing-profile` when on (driver 0.52.9 `serve --help`: "Pre-authorize a residual standard-mode boundary … existing-profile"; BROWSER.md "Existing profile": "For long-running service use, place `--grant existing-profile` on `cua-driver serve`"). Only on `serve`: the agents' `cua-driver mcp --socket` proxies connect to that daemon (`mcp --help`: its `--grant` is "for a newly launched runtime"). Off = exactly the old arguments. `cuaDriverServeArgs()` in `cua-driver-runtime.ts`; the switch is read at each daemon start (`existingChromeProfile` option), and `existingChromeProfileGranted()` reports the running daemon's grant.
  - No restart of a running driver on toggle: a new daemon announces a new MCP entry, which replaces every agent's provider session. So the change applies at the next start of OpenBot, and the panel says so (`restartNeeded` = driver running and its grant ≠ the saved switch).
  - Store: `openbot-computer-use-chrome-profile-v1.json` in userData (`{ version: 1, existingProfile }`, `PreferenceStore`). IPC `computerUse.getChromeProfile` / `setChromeProfile`. Local only: it drives this computer's Chrome.
  - Instructions (`developer-instructions.ts` `browserProfileRule`): off = the old `browser_consent_required … continue in openbot_browser` sentence, unchanged. On = select the exact Chrome window by title and size → `get_browser_state` → on `browser_consent_required`/`browser_requires_setup`, `browser_prepare {pid, window_id, strategy:{kind:"existing_profile"}}` without asking; `background_unavailable` → again with `delivery_mode` foreground; never click Chrome's prompt; one session label `chrome-<agent id prefix>` for that Chrome, no `end_session` for it; `openbot_browser` when the real profile is not needed. The flag is the running daemon's grant (`computerUseChromeProfile` through `agent-service.ts` → `thread-lifecycle.ts`), so the text always matches the tools.
- **How often Chrome asks:** once per new DevTools connection. The driver shares one approved connection between all sessions for the same Chrome: driver 0.52.9 `browser_prepare` description (binary string): "sessions for the same browser share one approved connection, kept for up to 30 minutes idle and 8 hours in total, so later sessions do not prompt again". It asks again after: Chrome restarts ("the browser process changed; existing-profile attachment needs fresh approval"), OpenBot (the driver) restarts (BROWSER.md: the grant "is discarded when the runtime shuts down"), 30 minutes with no use, or 8 hours. **Unverified:** BROWSER.md also says that ending the last Cua session for that Chrome turns remote debugging back off when Cua turned it on; a session ends after 5 minutes idle (RUNTIME.md) or when the agent's MCP connection closes, so Chrome may ask sooner than 30 minutes. If so, turning on remote debugging yourself once at `chrome://inspect/#remote-debugging` keeps it on (BROWSER.md: a session that attached to a setting the user enabled "does not claim ownership or turn it off"). OpenBot does not click the prompt, add Chrome flags, or edit Chrome's Preferences/Local State (BROWSER.md "Never").
- **Here:** `src/main/cua-driver-runtime.ts`, `src/main/computer-use-chrome-profile-store.ts`, `src/main/ipc/computer-use-handlers.ts`, `src/main/ipc/app-inputs.ts`, `src/main/application-services.ts`, `src/main/index.ts`, `src/preload/computer-use-decoding.ts`, `src/preload/index.ts`, `packages/contracts/src/ipc-app-auth.ts`, `packages/contracts/src/ipc-endpoints.ts`, `src/backend/agent/developer-instructions.ts`, `src/backend/agent/thread-lifecycle.ts`, `src/backend/agent-service.ts`, `src/renderer/src/features/computer-use/ComputerUseSetup.tsx`, `src/renderer/src/preview/mock-openbot.ts`, story `Settings/ComputerUseSetup/SettingsChromeProfile`, keys `computerUse.chromeProfile.*` (English only, in `untranslated.json`).
- **Tests:** vitest at Elias's request (upstream allows E2E only): `src/main/cua-driver-runtime.test.ts` (a real child process: off → `serve --socket <p> --no-overlay`; on → `+ --grant existing-profile`; switch read at each start), `src/backend/agent/developer-instructions.test.ts` (off text unchanged; on text; agent with Computer Use off gets none). `test:related` for the changed main files and for `developer-instructions.ts`: 11 files, all pass. `typecheck:node`, `typecheck:renderer`, `i18n:check`, Biome on changed files: clean. Storybook in headless Chrome: three fast clicks on the switch = one save (save slowed to 400 ms), "Saved" + restart note; shots `~/OpenBot/Shared/gotham/real-chrome/settings-*.png`.
- **Not covered:** a live attach to a real Chrome (needs the merged build and a restart); the real prompt frequency (see Unverified above); Windows/Linux attach.
- **Check:** turn the switch on, restart OpenBot, ask an agent "open my Gmail inbox in my Chrome and tell me the newest subject". Chrome shows "Allow remote debugging?" once; click Allow. Ask again within a few minutes: no prompt.

## 89. The chat pane never slides up: no header lost and no gap under the message box

- **Get:** while an agent works in a long chat, a jump to a late message (search, a notification, the dynamic island, a quote or reply link) moves only the transcript. The tab row stays at the top and the message box at the bottom. Before, the whole chat column slid up (up to the transcript's height): the header left the screen, the transcript started at the top edge, an empty band opened under the message box, and it stayed so until the chat was built again (the sidebar did not move).
- **Why:** Elias, 22 Nov 2026 (screenshot of Vegapunk's chat after a ⌘↩ send while it streamed): "there was this gap under the input box at the bottom that appeared very weirdly". Measured in Storybook: `.app-frame-panes.server-connection-workspace` (`overflow: hidden`) had 1,700–2,100 px of scrollable overflow while an agent worked, and a centred jump set its `scrollTop` to 190 (header `top` −190, gap 11 → 201 px). Probably the same as the 13 Nov "each chat jumps once and stays still" report.
- **How (portable):** the working line (`AgentActivity.tsx`) has a `.sr-only` live status, `position: absolute`. `.conversation-scroll` was `position: static`, so the status's containing block was `main.conversation-panel`: it escaped the transcript's clip and sat at the end of the transcript content, far under the window. That made the pane around the chat a scroll container with overflow. A scroll into view with `block: "center"`/`"start"` on a late row (`conversation-scope.ts` message focus, `search-store.ts`, `quoted-text.ts`, `AgentSteps.tsx`, …) scrolls every scroll container above it; the transcript stopped at its end, so the pane scrolled the rest. A pane with `overflow: hidden` takes no wheel, so it never came back. Fix: (1) `.conversation-scroll { position: relative }`: the transcript is the containing block of everything it scrolls; (2) guard: the pane and `.app-frame` are `overflow: clip` (not scroll containers, so no script or focus can scroll them), with `min-height: 0`, because a clipped grid item is not a scroll container and would grow to its content. Each part alone passes the test. The exact jump in Elias's session is not known; all `center`/`start` jumps to a late row had this effect.
- **Here:** `packages/ui/src/features/conversation/styles/timeline.css`, `src/renderer/src/features/usage/usage.css`, `src/renderer/src/styles/app-shell.css`; story `App` › "Agent works in long chat" (`App.stories.tsx`); `scripts/chat-pane-shift-e2e.ts`.
- **Tests:** `bun scripts/chat-pane-shift-e2e.ts --storybook=<url>` (centre the last message, then show the working line at the top; checks that no ancestor of the header scrolls or can scroll, the header stays at 0 and the gap under the message box stays the same). Before the fix: FAIL (pane `scrollTop` 190 then 666, header −190 / −666, gap 11 → 201 → 677 px). After: PASS (0, 0, 11 → 11). `scripts/chat-scroll-streaming-e2e.ts` "streaming while reading history" (agent and channel) fails the same way with and without the fix ("jumped up while a turn ran": a 20 px row change clamps `scrollTop` by 3 px); not caused by this change. Shots: `~/OpenBot/Shared/gotham/pane-shift/`.
- **Not covered:** the packaged app was not rebuilt or run; the web client and a separate chat window were not checked by hand.
- **Check:** in a long chat while the agent works, open a late message from search or a notification: the tab row stays at the top and the message box at the bottom.

## 91. Mark read on a folder

- **Get:** right-click a folder header ("Editing"): the menu starts with **Mark read** (the same double check icon as the agent menu). One click marks read every agent the folder shows and every agent in the folders inside it, at any level; the blue unread count on the closed folder goes away and so does each agent's badge. Agents with nothing unread, pinned agents (they show in the pinned strip) and agents in "Needs you" or the "Unread" group are not touched; nor are other folders. With nothing unread in the folder the item is not shown, as the agent menu shows Mark read only for an unread agent. Works on the "Unassigned" section's menu too.
- **Why:** Elias, 22 Nov 2026 (screenshots of the agent menu with Mark read and the folder menu without it): "let's add Mark read to group as well".
- **How (portable):** no new read model: the folder item calls the agent menu's Mark read callback once for each agent that is in the sidebar's unread set and in the chats the folder shows (its own rows plus its sub-folders', the same chats its badge counts). Scope per agent is exactly the agent Mark read's: on the desktop (local and joined server) its own chat through its newest message plus all its side threads (`markAgentChatRead`); in the web client its own chat (`workspace.markAgentRead`). Fast double click: the item decides whether it shows once when the menu opens (otherwise it would vanish during the close motion and Rename would slide under the pointer), marks once per opening (the reads are still in flight at the second click), and makes the list ignore clicks for 0.5 s (macOS double-click time), so the second click does not open the folder or chat under the closed menu.
- **Here:** `SectionMarkReadItem` in `packages/ui/src/features/sidebar/SidebarSectionHeader.tsx`; `sectionUnreadAgentIds` in `stores/data-store.ts` (via `sidebar-scope.ts`); `suppressSidebarClicks` in `createSidebarDragEngine.ts` (the drag engine's existing click guard). Label reuses `sidebar.agentMenu.markRead`. Story `Navigation/Sidebar` › `FoldersMarkRead`.
- **Tests:** `src/renderer/src/fork-folder-mark-read.test.tsx` (three-level folders, a side-thread-only unread agent, a pinned one, one in another folder: Mark read first, marks exactly the three, two fast clicks = one mark, a click under the menu does nothing, no rename, badge gone, item gone on reopen; other folder still offers it): 2/2 PASS, and fails without the click guard. `typecheck:renderer`, `typecheck:storybook`, `i18n:check` clean; Biome clean. Storybook in headless Chromium (`FoldersMarkRead`, 560×760 @2x): right-click closed "Clients" (badge 19) → Mark read → two real mouse clicks with no wait → 4 agents marked once each (incl. the side-thread-only one), badge gone, "Internal" keeps its 2 and stays closed, no rename editor, no page errors; open Clients: no unread badge on any row in Clients, Acme Robotics, Launch, Northwind; menu now without Mark read. Shots: `~/OpenBot/Shared/gotham/folder-mark-read/` (`1-before`, `2-menu`, `3-after`, `4-after-open*`, `5-menu-after`).
- **Not covered:** channels in a folder: their unread count is part of the folder badge, but no per-channel mark-read action exists, so they stay unread (only Mark all read clears them). The packaged app was not rebuilt or run. Mobile has no folder menu.
- **Check:** close a folder with unread agents, right-click its header, Mark read: the count goes away; open it: no agent badge.

## 92. An MCP or settings change waits for a Gemini (Antigravity) agent's subagents, and a stop is never silent

- **Get:** a Gemini (Antigravity) agent with running subagents keeps them when you turn an MCP server on or off, save or remove one, or change a skill or session setting: the chat says "⏳ The MCP change applies when its 5 subagents finish (at the latest in 30 min).", and the change is applied when the last one reports (or its turn ends). A change to a server that the agent cannot use does not touch its session at all. After 30 minutes the change is applied anyway. Whenever the process stops with subagents still running (that limit, a crash of the process, an account switch, a sign-out), each card shows "stopped" and the agent gets one wake: which subagents stopped (conversation ID, role, task), when, why, "do not wait for them", and to start only the work that is left again with `invoke_subagent`. Before, the process got SIGTERM at once, the subagents died with no word, and the agent said "5 subagents running" a minute later.
- **Why:** Elias, 22 Nov 2026 ("Yes, wait"; "if it restarts why is it stopping"), for Vegapunk: 5 `self` subagents started 19:41:57–19:42:54 all died at 19:43:08 (`Got signal terminated`, `acp-server-20261122_191005-74187.log:1738`), 8 ms after `servers:mcp:set-enabled` (#90).
- **Root cause:** `ThreadLifecycle.applyPendingRuntimeRefresh` (`thread-lifecycle.ts:1869` on `main`) waits only for a running turn or a client's `holdsBackgroundWork`; `AcpAgentClient` had none, so `#refreshThreadRuntime` → `releaseThread` → `#closeSession` (`acp-client.ts:1236`) stopped the session's own process (`sessionProcesses`), which runs its subagents. And every MCP row change refreshed every agent (`McpGateway.changed` → `refreshAllAgentRuntimes`). A process that ended by itself left the cards "running" (the per-session `exit` only set `needsResume`).
- **What restarts an agent's process (all wait now):** `refreshAllAgentRuntimes` (MCP save/remove/on-off, sign-in/out, sign-in app, a server's tools changed, MCP access per agent, Claude Code `~/.claude.json` servers, Computer Use, GitHub, a managed tool runtime ready) and `refreshAgentRuntime` (skill install/update/remove/on-off, session setting reset). Only MCP row changes (save, remove, on/off, `~/.claude.json`) are scoped to the agents whose set they change; sign-ins, Computer Use and GitHub still refresh every agent (their change does not show in the rows).
- **Resume after the restart (Elias's question) — not done, on evidence:** lab `~/Developer/lab/RE-antigravity-restart-resume/REPORT.md`, live on `antigravity acp` 1.5.14: a stopped child never restarts by itself (R1); resuming parent + child on a new process and prompting the PARENT to `send_message` the child continues it and its report wakes the parent (R2). But a resume with another `mcpServers` list only adds servers and starts the removed ones again from the conversation's own record (R3), so after an MCP change the agent must get a new session (as OpenBot does), and from it the old children are out of reach (their report goes to the old conversation). The crash path keeps the session, but continuing the child would also need OpenBot to open it again for its approvals (#84); left for later. So the wake has the agent restart the remaining work.
- **How (portable):**
  - `AgentClient.backgroundHoldLimitMs`, `runningBackgroundSubagents(threadId)` (new, optional). `AcpAgentClient.holdsBackgroundWork`: true while a subagent of a flavor with `subagentSessions` (Antigravity) runs (#90's cards); limit 30 min (`ACP_SUBAGENT_HOLD_LIMIT_MS`, the same as `ACP_BACKGROUND_WATCH_MS`; Antigravity ends a subagent only on `send_message` or a kill, so one that never reports would hold the change for good; test switch `OPENBOT_ACP_SUBAGENT_HOLD_LIMIT_MS`). Other ACP flavors return false (unchanged).
  - `ThreadLifecycle`: `#backgroundHold` per thread; a wait (`#backgroundWaits`, since/until) starts once, calls `hooks.refreshWaitsForBackground`, and after `until` the refresh closes the session anyway; a client with no limit (Claude, #46) waits as before. `refreshAgentRuntimesForMcpSet`: skips an agent whose open sessions were all given its current set (`#sessionMcpSets`, recorded at session start and resume from `#mcpGivenFingerprint`: all rows incl. Computer Use/GitHub, proxied ids, runtimes); any session without a record, not loaded, or a refresh already pending → refreshed as before.
  - `AgentService`: the chat line (keys `status.agent.refreshWaitsMcp` / `refreshWaitsSettings`, only when the client counts subagents and has a limit), a timer at `until`, and a new apply on each `turn/subagents/updated` or `turn/completed` of a waiting agent.
  - `AcpAgentClient`: `#closeSession`, `endBackgroundWork` and the per-session process `exit` send `FORK_BACKGROUND_STOPPED_METHOD` (cause `closed` / `crashed`, each subagent's card id, role, child conversation id, task) before the cards stop; the `exit` path now stops the cards too. Not at OpenBot's quit (`stop`).
  - `ForkRestartResume` (#47): for a provider other than Claude the wake does not need the same session (an MCP refresh gives an Antigravity chat a new one); Antigravity's own wake text (`antigravityResumeText`); its chat lines use new keys `status.agent.processSubagentResume*` with `{provider}` (the Claude lines are unchanged). English only (`untranslated.json`), as #47.
- **Here:** `src/backend/acp-client.ts`, `src/backend/agent-client.ts`, `src/backend/agent/thread-lifecycle.ts`, `src/backend/agent/mcp-gateway.ts`, `src/backend/agent-service.ts`, `src/backend/agent/turn-lifecycle.ts`, `src/backend/fork-restart-resume.ts`, `packages/i18n/src/messages/en/status/agent.ts`, `packages/i18n/untranslated.json`, `scripts/antigravity-restart-keeps-subagents-e2e.ts`, `scripts/provider-fakes/agents/antigravity-subagent.ts` (`spawn-hold-two`, `--version`, start/SIGTERM log), `package.json`.
- **Tests:** `bun run test:antigravity-restart-keeps-subagents` (real agent service, fake `agy acp`): `scoped-and-waits` (a server the agent cannot use → session kept; a used one while 2 subagents run → session and process kept, note "when its 2 subagents finish"; one reports → still kept; both → closed, SIGTERM, both "completed"), `limit-stops-and-tells` (limit 1.5 s → closed, both "stopped", one wake naming both conversations, roles and tasks, no `SendMessage`), `crash-stops-and-tells` (SIGKILL of the session process → both "stopped", wake "ended by itself"): 3/3 PASS; with `main`'s code all 3 FAIL (session closed at once; cards stay "running"). Also PASS: `test:antigravity-subagent-permissions` (#84/#90), `test:acp-subagents`, `test:antigravity-steer`, `test:antigravity-tool-filter`, `test:antigravity-failing-session`, `provider-session-reuse-e2e.ts` 6/6, `subagent-stop-resume-e2e.ts` 11/11, `routine-report-failure-e2e.ts`, `fork-restart-resume.test.ts` 11/11; `typecheck:node`, `typecheck:renderer`, `i18n:check`, Biome on changed files clean.
- **Other providers:** Claude: background subagents already hold every refresh with no limit (#46) and stop notices exist (#47, #72, #83); unchanged. Codex: `holdsBackgroundWork` counts background terminals only; its subagents are not checked (unverified). OpenCode (ACP): background subagents are tracked (`#heldByBackgroundSubagent`) but do not hold a refresh and get no notice: same gap, not fixed (no test). Kimi, Cline, Hermes: their subagents run inside the turn, which already holds the refresh.
- **Not covered:** a revoked MCP server stays in the running session up to 30 min (a trade-off of the wait). At OpenBot's quit the ACP client marks the cards "stopped" (as before), so the restart wake (#42) may not list them. A subagent continued in place after a crash (lab R2) is not done. The live app was not rebuilt or run.
- **Check:** ask a Gemini agent for two subagents that each run `sleep 120` and report; turn any MCP server it uses off: the chat says the change waits for 2 subagents; both report; then its next turn has the new set. Turn off a server it cannot use: nothing happens to it.

## 93. Gemini (Antigravity) question cards show each question with its options

- **Get:** when a Gemini (Antigravity) agent asks questions, the card has one page per question, with the question itself in large type and its options (A, B, C …); a multi-select question lets you tick several ("Choose all that apply"). A typed "Something else" answer goes back to the agent as that question's free answer. Before, the card showed "The agent has 3 questions." on 6 pages, with only "Something else" on each, and no options for a multi-select question.
- **Why:** Elias, 23 Nov 2026 (screenshot of Chopper's card: "The agent has 3 questions.", 6 page dots, one option "Something else").
- **Root cause:** live capture of `antigravity acp` 1.5.15 (lab `~/Developer/lab/RE-antigravity-ask-question/`): its `ask_question` tool sends one `elicitation/create` with `message: "The agent has %d questions."` and, per question, a field `q<n>` with `title` = the question and no `description` (single select: `type: "string"` + `oneOf: [{const: "1", title}]`; multi-select: `type: "array"` + `items.anyOf`), plus `q<n>_other` (`{type: "string", title: "Other", description: "Optional free-form answer to: …"}`); no `required`. `elicitationFieldQuestions` (`agent/prompts.ts:145` on `main`) used a field's description, else the request message, as the question, made a page of every field, and read options only from `oneOf`/`enum` at the top level.
- **How (portable, any ACP or MCP elicitation):**
  - Question text: the field's description, else its title. The request message is the question only of a one-field form without a description (MCP's usual "message asks, title names the field"); in a form of several fields it is the line under the first question (not under a multi-select, which keeps its own hint), unless it only counts the questions (`QUESTION_COUNT_LINE`: a short line ending in "N question(s)", Antigravity's "The agent has 3 questions."): the pages already show that, so no page has that grey line (the e2e checks every page's `detail`; it failed with "The agent has 3 questions." on page 1 before). Header: the title, cut to 40 characters at a word when the title is the question.
  - Options from `oneOf` or `anyOf` (`const` + `title`), or `enum` (+ `enumNames`); for `type: "array"` from `items` the same way, and the question is `multiSelect`. A field with choices is never taken for a secret by its name (a question title can say "token").
  - A string field `<id>_other` next to a choice field `<id>` makes no page: the card's typed answer on `<id>` fills `<id>_other`, picked labels become `<id>`'s consts (an array for a multi-select), and `<id>` is left out, or `[]` when the schema requires an array. Same mapping for ACP (`AcpAgentClient.#createElicitation`) and MCP (`mcpElicitationResult`), in `elicitationContent`.
- **Here:** `src/backend/agent/prompts.ts`, `src/backend/acp-client.ts`, `scripts/antigravity-ask-question-e2e.ts`, `scripts/provider-fakes/agents/antigravity-ask-question.ts`, `package.json`, story `Conversation/QuestionPromptBubble` › `AntigravityQuestions`.
- **Tests:** `bun run test:antigravity-ask-question` (real `antigravity` driver, fake `agy acp` 1.5.15 sending the captured request): 3 questions `q1`–`q3` with their own words and options, `q2` multi-select; picks → `{q1: "2", q2: ["2","3"], q3: "1"}`; typed → `{q1_other: "Purple", q2: ["1"], q2_other: "Mango", q3_other: "Zed"}`; no answer → `decline`. PASS; with `main`'s `src/backend` it FAILS (6 questions, "The agent has 3 questions.", no options on `q2`). Live: the probe answered `{q1_other, q2: ["2","3"], q3_other}` to the real 1.5.15 and the model read "Purple / Banana, Cherry / Zed". `test:mcp-tool-consent` (Codex 0.166.0): all cases incl. the secret API-key field and consents PASS. Its "sign-in by URL" step was stale (it expected the decline that upstream #11117 replaced with the sign-in page card) and failed on `main` before this too; it now checks the card (Continue → `accept`, "Don't sign in" → `decline`, no notice) and that a plain-http page still gets the notice and no card. A required single-select answered only by typing was declined (the MCP path counted the choice field as skipped); now its `<id>_other` answer counts for it and the request is accepted with the typed text there (`elicitationFieldResult`; e2e steps "required choice, picked / only typed" fail before, pass after): 29/29 PASS. `typecheck:node`, `typecheck:renderer`, `typecheck:storybook`, Biome on changed files clean. Story in headless Chrome @2x, real clicks (Banana clicked 3× fast = on): 3 dots, shots `~/OpenBot/Shared/gotham/ag-question-cards/`.
- **Not covered:** Chopper's already stored card keeps its old questions (it is answered as before). The packaged app was not rebuilt or run.
- **Check:** ask a Gemini agent to ask you a single-choice, a multiple-choice and an open question with its question tool: 3 pages with the real questions; type "Something else" on one: the agent gets your text.

## 94. A folder drags like a bot, and lands exactly where the drag shows, also between a folder's bots

- **Get:** dragging a folder header ("School", "Editing / Clients") looks and moves like dragging a bot: it lifts as the same 72×102 card (the folder icon in the avatar place, the folder name under it), the rows around it slide out of the way live (on the bot rows' timing and easing), and on release it settles into place with the bot drop's slide (200 ms, `cubic-bezier(0.2, 0, 0, 1)`) instead of jumping. Inside a folder, bots, channels and sub-folders can be in any order: drop a sub-folder above, between or below the folder's bots, or a bot above or between sub-folders, and it stays exactly there, also after a reload. The rest of the sweep: a folder dropped below the last folder goes last (it did nothing), a bot dropped back on its own row or its own folder's header stays put (it went to the end of the folder), a bot or folder held on a closed folder's header still springs it open and goes in, and the list no longer jumps to its end after a drop below the start.
- **Why:** Elias, 23 Nov 2026 (two recordings): a folder drag showed the row itself with no sliding while a bot drag had the card and the slide ("same lift/ghost, same live sliding, same drop/settle"); and "Editing / Clients" dropped above "Editors" and "Bot Bot" snapped back below them: "let me move/reorder a subfolder above specific channels/box while I'm inside a folder".
- **Root cause:** `startSectionDragging` used a clone of the header as the preview, and `sidebarDragOffsets` returned no offsets for `section-order` once folders nest (a drop line only); `sidebar-row-motion.ts` tracked only chat rows, so a dropped folder jumped. The layout had no order between a folder's chats and its folders: `SidebarAgentSection` drew all chats, then all sub-folders, and `folderTargetAtPoint` sent a folder dropped in its parent's rows `into` the parent (appended after the chats). Scroll anchoring read the rows held by the drop settle as moved content.
- **How (portable):**
  - Layout: `SidebarSection.beforeChatId` (optional): in its parent, the folder shows right before that chat; absent: after the chats. `sidebarFolderItems` (contracts) merges a folder's chats and folders by it; the renderer and the store share it. New local action `place` (`item`, `parentId`, `beforeAgentId`, `beforeSectionId`: the next chat and the next folder after the drop place): the store does the `move-agent` or `nest` and sets every sibling folder's `beforeChatId` from the new mixed order. Every action keeps the anchors valid: a folder whose chat leaves stands before the chat after it; a folder that changes parent goes after the chats. The file keeps the field (older versions drop it on load and show folders after the chats). The Team API contracts drop it, and the main-process remote path, the web client and the preview send `move-agent`/`nest` for `place` (`sidebarPlaceFallback`), so joined servers keep the old bots-then-folders order.
  - Drag: one path for both kinds. `startSectionDragging` uses the chat card (`createSidebarFolderDragCard` builds it through the same `createSidebarDragCard`), same class and size. `itemReorderOffsets` stacks a section's mixed rows (`stackReorderOffsets`: each row moves by the height of the dragged row, so an open folder with many bots works); used for chat and folder drags in their own section (no drop line then; a move to another section keeps the line or the blue outline). A folder drag resolves against the rows where they show (slots shifted by the offsets; over its own moved gap it keeps the target). In a folder's rows a dragged folder or bot takes a place before/after the row under the pointer (`chatTargetInSection`, `folderTargetAtPoint`); on a sub-folder's header the top quarter puts it before, the bottom quarter of a closed one after, the rest in. `sidebar-row-motion.ts` tracks sections too (`[data-section-id]`), plays each row's move less its section's, and holds a dropped folder under the pointer by its header until the new order is in. `overflow-anchor: none` while dragging and settling.
- **Here:** `packages/contracts/src/ipc-sidebar-layout.ts`, `src/backend/sidebar-layout-store.ts`, `src/main/ipc/agent-inputs.ts`, `src/main/ipc/agent-handlers.ts`, `src/renderer/src/features/web-client/web-runtime.ts`, `src/renderer/src/preview/mock-sidebar-layout.ts`, `packages/ui/src/features/sidebar/` (`createSidebarDragEngine.ts`, `sidebar-drag-model.ts`, `sidebar-drag-dom.ts`, `sidebar-row-motion.ts`, `SidebarAgentSection.tsx`, `sidebar-scope.ts`, `stores/data-store.ts`, `stores/drag-state-store.ts`, `stores/layout-actions.ts`), `src/renderer/src/styles/fork-sidebar.css`, `scripts/sidebar-folder-reorder-e2e.ts`.
- **Tests:** `bun scripts/sidebar-folder-reorder-e2e.ts` (real layout store on a temp profile, order read with `sidebarFolderItems`): top-level reorder + reload, drop in place keeps the revision, sub-folder above / between the chats (the recording) + reload, chat above a sub-folder and between two, chat from another folder at the very top and bottom, a chat leaving keeps the folders' places, a folder moved to another folder drops its anchor, the file holds the anchor, the `place` fallback: PASS. `sidebar-chat-reorder-e2e.ts`, `sidebar-folders-e2e.ts` (frozen Team API contract), `fork-sidebar-drag-motion.test.ts`, `fork-folder-drop-outline.test.ts`: PASS. `typecheck:node`, `typecheck:renderer`, Biome on changed files clean. Dev instance (own worktree profile, 18 bots, 7 folders incl. "Editing / Clients", "Editing / Archive", an 8-bot folder), real HTML5 drags over CDP: bot vs folder side by side (same card, slide, settle), sub-folder above the bots (stays, persists after reload), expanded 8-bot folder moved up, closed folder moved down, fast 2-step drag to the top, drop back in place (no change), drop below the last folder, bot above a sub-folder and at the top of a folder, bot and folder into closed folders (spring open). Clips and strips: `~/OpenBot/Shared/gotham/folder-drag/`.
- **Not covered:** joined servers and the web client keep chats before folders (Team API contracts are frozen); mobile shows chats before folders. The packaged app was not rebuilt or run. Drags were driven over CDP (`Input.dispatchDragEvent`), not the macOS pointer: Cua Driver refused a background drag on this Electron window (`electron_main_process_unavailable`).
- **Check:** in a folder with two bots and a sub-folder, drag the sub-folder above the first bot: the bots slide down under the card, it stays above them, and is still there after a restart.

## 95. Replies stay in a long turn: no earlier answer leaves the chat, for a moment or for good

- **Get:** an agent's replies in a long turn stay in the chat while a new reply streams, when the agent's state changes, when you open the chat again, and when you scroll back. Before, a turn of more than 100 messages (a Gemini agent that you steer for hours) lost its replies: all of them, or a batch, went for a moment and came back with the next change, and a chat that opened or scrolled back showed only your own messages.
- **Why:** Elias, 23 Nov 2026 (screenshot of Vegapunk: only his "⏳ Waits for the current step" messages, "Coordinating with another agent…"); "many earlier replies disappeared at once, temporarily", and still "random batches keep disappearing and reappearing". His thread lost nothing: 2,388 replies stored, none deleted, the stored rows did not change during the flicker.
- **Root cause (renderer, not storage):** the desktop chat keeps converted rows, and all commentary of a turn is one steps row (`thinking:<turn>`) whose items are the turn's replies (`toAgentMessages`). A host page holds whole turns up to 50 messages and only the end of a turn of more than 100 (`wholeGroups`, `conversation-queries.ts`). Vegapunk's turn `b38554fb…` ran from 09:45 with 154 rows and a queued reply from Explorer after it, so its latest page was that one message, which the chat does not show. Four places then replaced what the chat had with a part:
  1. Every agent state change, chat open and connection change reads the latest page and **replaced** the chat with it (`applyConversationPage(page, "replace", "latest")`, `conversation-context.tsx:299` on `main`): the chat was empty until the next snapshot, or showed the end of the turn only.
  2. A page or snapshot that holds a part of a turn built a steps row of that part, and it **replaced** the whole row by id (`updateStored`): every reply before that part went.
  3. A capped snapshot starts the window at the first loaded message it shares (`windowedSnapshotMessages`); the steps row stands at the turn's first step, so a window that started later in the turn cut it off, with all replies; a steps row counted as that first message and cut the turn's loaded start too.
  4. An older page went in front of all loaded rows even when a snapshot had already loaded older ones, so the turn's end showed above the older turns and the next snapshot cut them.
  The ACP labels are not the cause: ACP cannot tell the answer while it streams, so the newest text streams as `final_answer` and becomes `commentary` at the next step (`acp-client.ts` `#completeMessage`), and a Gemini turn that takes steers stays open. Unchanged.
- **How (portable):**
  - `conversation-merge.ts`: `pageWindow` (the page merge, moved out of the context and pure), `pageMeetsLoaded` (a latest page meets the loaded latest window when it shares a row, shows nothing, starts before the last loaded row, or the chat already has its revision): such a page **joins** the chat as the web client and the phone do, instead of replacing it; a page after a gap still replaces it. `mergeOlderPage` puts each new row of an older page in its place among the loaded ones (steps rows place nothing). `windowedSnapshotMessages` starts at the whole turn of the first shared message and skips steps rows when it looks for that message. `snapshotRows` / `pageRows` add the loaded items of a turn that the source does not reach.
  - `app-message-projection.ts`: `turnCoverage`, `withUnreachedTurnSteps` (an item outside the time of the turn's messages in the source stays; one inside it that the source lacks was removed and goes), `placeTurnStepRows` (a steps row goes before the first message of its turn that is newer than its first item; a list in the shown order does not change).
  - `conversation-context.tsx`: uses them for snapshots, pages and `keepOlderHistoryPlace`; a waiting snapshot that is not newer than a page applies before it; the trim of a chat that is not open keeps the steps row of a kept turn.
  - Web client: `pageMeetsLoaded` for its `apart` check. Phone (`apps/mobile` `MobileConversationStore.loadLatest`): a latest page that does not meet the loaded window reads up to 3 older pages to reach it (the reads pass `before=`), else replaces it as before.
- **Here:** `src/renderer/src/features/conversation/conversation-merge.ts`, `src/renderer/src/app-message-projection.ts`, `src/renderer/src/features/conversation/conversation-context.tsx`, `src/renderer/src/features/web-client/web-client-context.tsx`, `apps/mobile/src/features/workspace/model/conversation-store.ts`, `apps/mobile/src/features/workspace/context/mobile-workspace-context.tsx`.
- **Tests:** `src/renderer/src/fork-long-turn-replies.test.ts` (a host that cuts pages and snapshots as the backend does; a chat store with the same steps; checks after every step that no row that showed has gone): Antigravity, OpenCode, Claude and Codex item ids; open on a one-message latest page, a step and a reply that streams as the answer, a state change while it streams, the reply becoming commentary, scroll back page by page with snapshots between, a steer, a joined server's page event, the turn's end; the steps row whole after a partial page; whole order after scrolling back; a finished normal turn unchanged (steps folded above the answer). 16/16 PASS; with `main`'s merge code 9 of the first 12 FAIL ("latest page while the reply streams: 109 earlier rows left the chat"), and the 4 scroll-back cases caught a first version of this fix that put the turn's start after its replies. `src/backend/fork-long-turn-page.test.ts` (the real `readConversationPage`: 100 rows of a 151-row turn; only the queued reply after it). `apps/mobile/.../conversation-store.test.ts` 2/2 (FAIL before). Replay of a read-only copy of his database (`~/OpenBot/Shared/gotham/vanished-replies/vegapunk-real-data-replay.txt`): before, a state change showed 0 rows, an older page 25 of 37 replies, the next snapshot 69 of 113 rows; after, 113 rows and 37 replies at every step. `typecheck:renderer`, `typecheck:node`, `typecheck:storybook`, `typecheck:mobile`, Biome on changed files clean.
- **Dev instance:** sandbox profile and HOME, a seeded Vegapunk-like chat (3 turns, a 151-row turn with 60 replies and 30 steers, a teammate reply after it), launched in the background with cua-driver: `main` opened on only the steers (the screenshot he sent); the fix shows every reply, scrolled back the whole turn in order, and after switching chats and back. Shots in `~/OpenBot/Shared/gotham/vanished-replies/`.
- **Not covered:** a turn that streams for real in the dev instance (no provider there; covered by the tests). A message deleted at the very edge of a page's part of a turn stays until a page holds the whole turn.
- **Check:** steer a Gemini agent many times in one turn until it has more than 100 messages; switch to another chat and back, scroll to the top and back, and let it start a new reply: no earlier reply leaves the chat.

## 96. Subfolders sit on a tree line

- **Get:** inside an open folder, everything in it (its bots, channels, boxes and subfolders) is indented 16 px, with a thin muted guide line from under the folder header down its content, as in Finder or VS Code. A subfolder shows only its own name ("Clients", not "Editing / Clients"); each deeper level repeats the step with its own line, up to the 3 folder levels. Closed folders, pinned bots, "Needs you" and "Unread" are unchanged. The header tooltip, the region label, Move to and rename still use the full path / the folder's own record. While a filter (e.g. Unread) shows a folder only for a subfolder in it, that folder has no header (upstream), so it does not indent either and the subfolder names its parent first again ("Engineering / Archive"). At 210 px wide and below (where rows drop their time) a step is 10 px. A dragged bot over a closed or empty folder now shows the whole blue outline (its bottom edge was missing).
- **Why:** Elias, 23 Nov 2026: picked mock "A — tree line" of three subfolder looks (`~/OpenBot/Shared/gotham/subfolder-mockups/A-tree-line*.png`); upstream's folders do not indent and prefix the parent name, so nested folders were hard to read.
- **How (portable):** CSS only for the look: `.sidebar:not(.sidebar-compact) .sidebar-folder:not([data-headless]) > .sidebar-section-collapse > .sidebar-section-body` gets `padding-inline-start: var(--sidebar-tree-indent)` (16 px, 10 px in a `@container (max-width: 210px)`), and its `::before` is the 1 px `--openbot-border-strong` line at `calc(indent / 2 - 1px)` (7 px: in line with the folder name), 4 px from the top and bottom, `pointer-events: none`. The indent lives on the body, so rows, subfolder headers, their sticky headers, the drag lift gap, drop lines and the drop outline all follow it, and #94's mixed order (subfolders between bots) needs nothing more. `SidebarAgentSection` marks a folder without a header `data-headless`; `SidebarSectionHeader` shows upstream's muted `parent / ` prefix only when the parent is headless (`listFiltered()` and the parent shows no chats). The drop outline fix: on a drop-target folder whose collapse is `[data-collapsed]` or whose body is `:empty`, the header's `::before` draws all four sides.
- **Here:** `src/renderer/src/styles/fork-sidebar.css` (end), `packages/ui/src/features/sidebar/SidebarAgentSection.tsx` (`data-headless`), `packages/ui/src/features/sidebar/SidebarSectionHeader.tsx` (`parentHeadless`). `app-shell.css` untouched.
- **Tests:** `src/renderer/src/fork-folder-tree-line.test.tsx` (three levels + an empty subfolder: own names, full-path tooltip and label, nesting; Rename pre-fills the own name; Unread filter: parents headless, "Clients / Deep"; CSS rules for indent, line, compact/headless exclusion, closed/empty outline): 6/6 PASS, the two markup tests fail on the pre-feature components. `fork-folder-mark-read`, `fork-folder-drop-outline`, `fork-sidebar-drag-motion`, `vitest related` on the two components, `bun scripts/sidebar-folder-reorder-e2e.ts` (10/10), `typecheck:node`, `typecheck:renderer`, `check:ui`, Biome: PASS. Hidden DEV instance (own throwaway profile, local auth API, fake CLIs; 33 bots, 3 levels, long names, 16-bot folder), shot over CDP at 1745×982 @2.2 (~110 % zoom), real clicks and HTML5 drags: 3 levels, empty subfolder, closed subfolder with unread badge (badge + icon right-aligned), 392/300/200/180 px widths, sticky nested headers while scrolling, selected + hover rows, Unread group order, pinned strip, context menu (Mark read on a level-3 folder, Rename → layout file has the new own name, tooltip has the path), Unread filter, parent collapse + 3 fast clicks, bot into an empty subfolder, subfolder → subfolder, subfolder → parent, folder dragged between two bots (`beforeChatId` saved), outline on closed and empty folders. Shots: `~/OpenBot/Shared/gotham/subfolder-tree-line/`.
- **Not covered:** "Needs you" was not seeded (no waiting agent); it shares the "Unread" group's markup, which shows unchanged. Channels in a folder were not seeded (same row markup and body as bots). The glass (transparent) window and the packaged app were not run. Mobile has no folder tree.

## 97. A second provider account's picker shows only that account's models

- **Get:** the model picker of a second account (e.g. Gemini "2", blikkolol) lists only the models that this account's CLI lists: the shared models that the account also has, plus its own extra models. A model that only the first account has is left out, so a name no longer shows twice (Elias's "Orchestrator (Argon)" ×2). The model that the agent uses now stays in the list even when the account does not list it, so its check mark shows. The first account, a pool, and accounts whose list is not read yet are unchanged.
- **Why:** Elias, 23 Nov 2026, screenshot of Chopper's picker on Gemini blikkolol: "Orchestrator (Neon)", "Orchestrator (Argon)" twice, "Orchestrator (Carbon)", "Gemini 4 Carbon".
- **Root cause (upstream, not the v0.76.1 merge):** `ProviderModelPicker` `entryModelOptions` showed the provider's shared list (the first account's, `~/.gemini`) plus the account's `models`, and `ProviderRuntime.#extraModels` sends for an account only the models the shared list lacks. Antigravity 1.5.15 gives the two Google accounts different lists (`session/new` probe on temp copies of both homes): the first has 9 models incl. `gemini-pro-orchestrator-g1-pro` "Orchestrator (Argon)"; blikkolol has 11 incl. `gemini-pro-orchestrator-argon` "Orchestrator (Argon)", `gemini-pro-orchestrator` "Orchestrator (Carbon)" and `gemini-4-carbon`, but not `g1-pro`. So the picker showed the first account's `g1-pro` (not runnable there) and blikkolol's own Argon. "Older models 2" are Gemini 4 Argon/Hydrogen (not in the release catalogue, `model-visibility.ts:501`); Sonnet 5.5 and GPT-OSS are hidden in `openbot-model-visibility-v1.json`. The "Orchestrator" names come from Google's own list.
- **How (portable):** `ProviderAccountStatus.missingModels?: AgentModelId[]` (optional; validated in `isProviderAccountStatus`). `withAccountModelDifference(account, catalogue)` (new `src/backend/agent/fork-account-missing-models.ts`) replaces `#extraModels`' body: `models` = the account's extras as before, `missingModels` = shared ids that the account's non-empty list lacks. `accountModelOptions(shared, account, inUse)` in `provider-model-options.ts` filters the shared list by it (keeps `inUse` = the current agent model on the active key) and appends the extras; `entryModelOptions` calls it. Older clients ignore the field.
- **Here:** `packages/contracts/src/ipc-agent-status.ts`, `src/backend/agent/provider-runtime.ts`, `src/backend/agent/fork-account-missing-models.ts`, `packages/ui/src/components/provider-model-options.ts`, `packages/ui/src/components/ProviderModelPicker.tsx`, story `Conversation/ProviderModelPicker` › `SecondGeminiAccount`.
- **Tests:** `src/backend/agent/fork-account-missing-models.test.ts` (3) and `src/renderer/src/fork-account-missing-models.test.ts` (3) with Elias's two lists; `test:antigravity-accounts` e2e, `src/preload/agent-decoding.test.ts`, `typecheck:node`, `typecheck:renderer`, `typecheck:storybook`, Biome: PASS. Story shot in headless Chrome, before (old `entryModelOptions`: Argon twice) and after: `~/OpenBot/Shared/gotham/screenshot-bugs-1123/picker-before.png`, `picker-after.png`.
- **Not covered:** the mobile app's account model list (`agent-runtime-fields.tsx`), Backup models and the agent's `list_models` tool still add the shared list per account. The visibility catalogue (older/hidden) still does not apply to an account's extra models (upstream). Not run in the packaged app.
- **Check:** open the picker of an agent on Gemini account 2: one "Orchestrator (Argon)", and "Gemini 4 Carbon" / "Orchestrator (Carbon)" listed; account 1 shows its own 9.

## 98. Math in a table cell is typeset: matrices keep their row breaks

- **Get:** `$…$` and `$$…$$` in a chat table cell show as typeset math, also a matrix (`pmatrix`, `bmatrix`) with `\\` row breaks and `&` columns. `\{`, `\}`, `\frac` and `\|` stay as written in the formula. A `\|` in a formula does not split the cell. Text, escapes and code spans outside the formula show as before. "Copy as Markdown" gives the formula as written.
- **Why:** Elias, 24 Nov 2026, screenshot of Owl's reply: a table cell showed the raw `$\begin{pmatrix}1&-2&-1\-1&1&0\-1&0&-1\end{pmatrix}$` in place of the matrix; the same matrix in a `$$…$$` block out of a table was correct.
- **Root cause:** a reply's top-level table is parsed by `parseRow` in `packages/ui/src/features/conversation/DataTable.tsx` (not by marked). It removed the backslash from each `\\` and `\|` in all of the cell, so the cell's inline Markdown pass got `\-1` in place of `\\-1`, KaTeX failed and `MathFormula` showed the source. marked's own table (`MarkdownMessageText`, used for tables in quotes, user messages and Markdown previews) keeps `\\` but changes `\|` to `|`, as GFM does.
- **How (portable):** split a table row on unescaped `|` (as before, so an unescaped `|` in a formula still splits the cell, as in GFM). Then in each cell, find the formulas with the chat's own inline math rule, skip code spans, keep each formula verbatim and unescape only the text around it. Copy as Markdown does the reverse: escape only the text around the formulas. For marked tables, a `table` tokenizer override puts back the `\|` in formulas and lexes that cell again, only when the row has a `\|` and the cell lines up with marked's own cell.
- **Here:** `packages/contracts/src/chat-table-math.ts` (`@openbot/contracts/chat-table-math`: `splitTableRow`, `mapTextOutsideMath`, `keepMathInTableCells`; moved from `packages/ui/src/features/conversation/table-cell-math.ts` so the phone uses the same code); `parseRow` and `dataTableMarkdown` in `DataTable.tsx`; the `table` tokenizer in `MarkdownMessageText.tsx`. The web client and the desktop with a joined server use the same components. The mobile app (`apps/mobile`) has the same `table` tokenizer in `apps/mobile/src/features/chat/model/chat-markdown-parser.ts`, so a phone table cell keeps `\|` in a formula too (finished and streaming replies). Story `Conversation/MessageBody` › `DataTableMath`.
- **Not like GFM:** in GFM (and on GitHub) `$\|v\|$` in a table cell becomes `|v|`; here it stays `\|v\|` (‖v‖), as Elias asked. Write `\vert` for a single bar.
- **Tests:** `src/renderer/src/features/conversation/MarkdownTableMath.test.tsx`, 19 tests for a reply's table (`MessageBody`) and a marked table (`MarkdownMessageText`): pmatrix, bmatrix, `\frac` with a matrix, `\{…\|v\|…\}`, two formulas in one cell, `$$…$$` in a cell, `\\` text next to a formula, escaped text without math, an unescaped `|` still splits, Copy as Markdown. Before the fix 9 failed, 10 passed; after the fix 19 passed. `vitest related` on the three sources (5 files, 35 tests), `typecheck:renderer`, `typecheck:storybook`, Biome: PASS. A fuzz of 300 000 random rows: the old and new `parseRow` give the same cells unless a formula holds `\\` or `\|`. Story shot in headless Chromium, before and after: `~/OpenBot/Shared/gotham/table-math/before-fix-dark.png`, `after-fix-dark.png`. Mobile (24 Nov 2026, chat bug hunt): `apps/mobile/src/features/chat/model/chat-markdown-parser.test.ts`, 12 tests on the phone's parser for a finished and a streaming reply (`\{…\|v\|…\}` keeps its bars and does not split the cell, pmatrix, two formulas, `$$…$$`, `\|` outside the formula and in a code span unescaped as before, an unescaped `|` still splits): before the port 8 failed, 4 passed; after it 12 passed. The 19 desktop tests still pass.
- **Not covered:** the phone's table typesetting was not seen on a device (the parser tokens are tested). A formula that an emphasis or link opened before it and closes inside it is not found as marked finds it. Not run in the packaged app.
- **Check:** ask an agent for a table with `$\begin{pmatrix}1&2\\3&4\end{pmatrix}$` in a cell: the cell shows a 2×2 matrix.

## 99. Chat flow: live states that showed wrong or went stale

- **Get:** the chat's live parts say what is true: the work bar under the header, the day separators, the steps rows and the queued-message list.
- **Why:** bug hunt of the conversation flow, 24 Nov 2026 (Elias asked for every bug in how a chat renders over time, each with a failing test, a fix and proof). Shots: `~/OpenBot/Shared/gotham/chat-bughunt/flow/`.
- **Fixes:**
  - **"0 working" in the work bar.** A question or an approval that waits, with no subagent and no plan running, showed the bar as "0 working · 1 waits for you" (`LiveWork.tsx`: the title counted only subagents and plans). Now the bar shows only "1 waits for you"; with work it still says "N working". Test: `src/renderer/src/fork-chat-flow-live-work.test.tsx` (fails on the old bar). Shots: `live-work-zero-working-before.png`, `-after.png`.
  - **Steps row without its day separator.** A turn that ended with no answer (stopped, failed) shows only its "N steps" row. When that row was the first row of a new day, or the first row after a context compaction, the chat drew no day separator and no "Context compacted" note there (`ConversationTimeline.tsx`: the steps-row branch skipped both), and the next row did not draw the separator either, as it is on the same day. So after midnight the chat went on under yesterday's date. The steps row now draws them as every other row does (the side-thread view already did). Test: `src/renderer/src/fork-chat-flow-timeline.test.tsx` (the full chat view in jsdom; the day test fails on the old timeline). Story `Conversation/Conversation` › `StoppedTurnAfterMidnight`. Shots: `steps-row-day-separator-before.png`, `-after.png`.
  - **Day separators kept yesterday's names.** A separator says "Today 22:00" or "Yesterday 23:58" from the date when its row was drawn. The chat never drew it again, so a chat left open over midnight (OpenBot runs all night) kept "Today" on yesterday's rows, and the first row after midnight got a second "Today" separator. `createChatDayClock` (`chat-day-markers.ts`) checks the date once a minute (so also within a minute after the Mac wakes) and changes only when the day changes; the main chat and the side-thread view give it to `dayMarkerLabel` as `now`. Test: `fork-chat-flow-timeline.test.tsx` › "renames the day separators…" (fake clock from 23:59 to 00:01; fails on the old timeline: "Today 10:00 PM" stays). Shots (Playwright clock at 00:01 after opening at 23:59): `day-separator-midnight-before.png` ("Yesterday 11:58 PM", "Today 12:01 AM"), `-after.png` ("Fri, Nov 20 11:58 PM", "Yesterday 12:01 AM").
  - **Queued messages lost focus on Alt+↑/↓.** The queued-message list keyed its rows by object, and every queue snapshot from the host has new objects, so each change made every row again (`QueuePanel.tsx`). After one Alt+↑ the moved row was a new element, focus went to the page, and the next Alt+↑ did nothing; a row's "Editing" state was also read once when the row was made. Rows are now keyed by delivery id and read the delivery as it changes, and after a keyboard move the moved row gets focus back when the new order arrives (only when focus was lost or is still in the list). Test: `src/renderer/src/fork-chat-flow-queue.test.tsx` (fails on the old list: focus on `body` after the first move). Browser check on story `SevenQueuedMessages` (row 3, Alt+↑ twice): before, focus `BODY` and the second press did nothing; after, the row moves to the top with focus on it. Evidence: `queue-keyboard-reorder.json`, `queue-keyboard-reorder-before.png`, `-after.png`.
  - **Chinese and Japanese replies froze while they streamed.** The reveal of a streaming reply shows a word only when a space follows it (`streamingReveal.ts`, `STREAMING_WORD`), so it never shows half a word or half a link. Chinese and Japanese have no spaces between words, so a reply in them showed nothing of a paragraph until the line ended or the reply ended, then all of it at once. A Han, Hiragana or Katakana character now also ends a step; English text and links without spaces stream as before. Test: `src/renderer/src/fork-chat-flow-streaming.test.tsx` (renders `MessageBody` with a growing reply on a fake clock; the Chinese case fails on the old reveal, the English case passes on both).
  - **A long teammate name ran over "Working" in "Waiting for replies".** The name in each row of the card never shrank (`awaiting-replies.css`, `flex: 0 0 auto`), so a long agent name in a narrow chat (a side panel open, a small window) ran under the state label and out of the card. The name now ends with "…" when the row is full, and the reply preview gives way first, so a short name stays whole. Story `Conversation/Awaiting replies` › `LongTeammateName`. Test: a layout check in headless Chromium at 520 px (`awaiting-long-name-measure.mjs` in the shots folder; jsdom has no layout): before, the name's right edge 515 px over the state label at 423 px; after, 411 px and "Research" not cut. Shots: `awaiting-long-name-before.png`, `-after.png`.
- **Not covered:** the packaged app was not rebuilt or run; the fixes were checked in jsdom tests and Storybook (headless Chromium).
- **Check:** let an agent ask you a question: the bar under the header says "1 waits for you", without "0 working".

## 100. Chat text in the phone, the web client and the short previews: bug hunt fixes

- **Get:** the phone, the web client and every one-line preview (sidebar, notifications, Dynamic Island, search, "Needs you") show a chat message as the desktop chat does, or as clean one-line text: no raw Markdown marks, no cut emoji.
- **Why:** Elias, 24 Nov 2026: bug hunt of chat rendering in the other clients and the short text previews, after the table-math fix (#98).
- **Fixes** (one commit each; tests named per fix):
  - **Phone: a tagged attachment showed a stray `@`.** A user message that tags a file (`@[report.pdf](attachment:id)`, from the desktop composer) showed `@report.pdf` on the phone; the desktop shows `report.pdf` (or its chip). Cause: the phone's parser (`apps/mobile/src/features/chat/model/chat-markdown-parser.ts`) had no attachment-tag rule, so marked read `@` as text and the rest as a link with an `attachment:` address, which the phone shows as its label. Fix: an `attachmentTag` inline rule gives the file name as text. Test: `chat-markdown-parser.test.ts` › "a tagged attachment in a message" (failed before, passes after).
  - **Sidebar, Dynamic Island and phone list: a long reply's preview stopped inside its Markdown.** The server keeps an agent's last reply as its preview, cut at 180 characters (`agent-store.ts` `updatePreview`), and the runtime snapshot cuts the last reply and a question at 240 (`runtime-snapshot.ts`). The cut was `String.slice`: it could stop inside `**bold**`, `$$…$$`, a link or a code span, so the stripped preview showed `**`, `$$\begin{pmatrix}…`, `[label](https://…` or a backtick, and it could cut an emoji in half (a lone surrogate shows as �). Fix: `markdownPreviewSource` (`packages/contracts/src/markdown-preview-text.ts`) cuts at whole characters (`textPrefix`, `Intl.Segmenter`) and goes back word by word until the cut's plain text is the start of the full reply's plain text, so no Markdown span stays open. When that leaves less than half the room, it gives the reply's plain text, cut to the limit. A cut preview ends with `…` (the phone's peek bubble used to tell a cut preview by its length of 180). Repro: a reply of 150 characters of text, then `**The root cause is that the cache drops writes.**`: the sidebar line ended with `**The root cause is th`. Test: `src/backend/fork-preview-cut.test.ts` (11 tests: bold, display math, link, code span, ellipsis, no ellipsis when the whole text fits, emoji, five shapes under the limit, a question in the runtime snapshot; 10 failed with the old cut, all pass now).
  - **Notifications: the text could end inside bold text, a link or a formula.** A macOS or phone notification for a reply shows a summary of whole sentences (`messageSummary`, `packages/team-client/src/message-summary.ts`), then strips its Markdown. The sentence split did not look at Markdown (only at backticks), so a full stop inside `**…**`, `[…](…)` or `$…$` could end the summary there and the notification showed `… services. **Root cause.` or `See [the design doc.` Fix: one inline lex of the paragraph (`markdownSpanRanges`) gives the Markdown spans, and the split skips a full stop inside one. The Live Activity uses the same summary. Test: `src/main/fork-notification-preview.test.ts` (4 tests; bold, link and math failed before, all pass now).
  - **Dynamic Island: a reply, a question and a working task showed raw Markdown.** The island's "replied" panel showed the reply as written (`## Summary **Done.** Run \`bun test\` … <br>$$x^2$$`), the question panel showed `**` and backticks, and a working row showed a tagged file or agent as `@[report.pdf](attachment:…)`. Cause: `OpenBotDynamicIsland.tsx` put `message().text`, the question and `item().task` in the description as plain strings, and the presentation (`dynamic-island-presentation.ts`) only trims them. Fix: the three descriptions go through `markdownPreviewText` (as the sidebar does). The presentation keeps the Markdown, because the Live Activity renders it. Test: `src/renderer/src/features/dynamic-island/fork-island-preview-text.test.tsx` (3 tests, all failed before, all pass now).
  - **"Needs you" rows (desktop sidebar, chat's waiting bar, phone) showed a question's raw Markdown.** When an agent asks "Which **deploy target** should I use: \`prod\` or [staging](…)?", the sidebar row under "Needs you", the waiting bar over the chat and the phone's wait line showed the `**`, backticks and link syntax. Cause: the wait detail was the question trimmed, with no Markdown pass (`questionWait`/`approvalWait` in `packages/ui/src/features/sidebar/sidebar-agent-states.ts`, `questionDetail`/`approvalDetail` in `apps/mobile/src/features/workspace/model/agent-activity.ts`). Fix: the question and an approval's reason go through `markdownPreviewText`; an approval's command stays as written. Tests: `src/renderer/src/fork-needs-you-preview.test.ts` (2) and `apps/mobile/src/features/workspace/model/agent-activity.test.ts` (1); all failed before, all pass now.
  - **Desktop search (⌘K): a found message showed its raw Markdown.** A message result's line showed `## Deploy notes **Root cause:** … [the report](https://…)<br> $$\begin{pmatrix}…$$ \`\`\`ts …`; the phone's search already strips it. Cause: `messagePreview` in `packages/ui/src/components/GlobalSearch.tsx` only expanded tags and joined the lines. Fix: it uses `markdownPreviewText` (which also expands tags and quotes). Test: `src/renderer/src/fork-search-snippet.test.tsx` (2 tests; the Markdown one failed before, both pass now).
  - **Notification and search snippet: a cut could split an emoji.** A notification longer than 200 characters is cut by code points (`previewText` in `packages/team-client/src/agent-notifications.ts`), so a family, flag or skin-tone emoji at the cut lost its parts (`👨‍👩‍` …). A search snippet that starts inside a long word (CJK, emoji runs) started at a raw UTF-16 index (`snippetAround` in `packages/ui/src/components/global-search-text.ts`, `mobileSearchSnippet` in `apps/mobile/src/features/search/model/mobile-search.ts`), so it could start with half an emoji (�). Fix: both cut at a grapheme boundary (`textPrefix`). Tests: `src/renderer/src/fork-preview-characters.test.ts` (2) and `apps/mobile/src/features/search/model/mobile-search.test.ts` (1); all failed before, all pass now.
  - **Phone: an agent's citations showed raw in the chat.** A Codex reply with a file citation (`:codex-file-citation{path="/repo/src/app.ts" line_range_start=3}`) or a search citation (private-use marks `\u{e200}cite\u{e202}turn0search1\u{e201}`) showed that text (and empty boxes) in the phone's bubble and work steps; the desktop and the web client show a file link and drop the search mark (`cleanAgentMessageText`, `app-message-projection.ts`). Cause: the phone's projection (`apps/mobile/src/features/chat/model/chat-messages.ts`) used `message.text` as it came from the host and never called `cleanAgentMessageText`. Fix: agent bubbles, progress bubbles and work steps go through `cleanAgentMessageText`; the user's own text stays as written. Test: `apps/mobile/src/features/chat/model/chat-messages.test.ts` (3 tests; the reply and the work step failed before, all pass now). The test maps the phone's three `@/` imports to their real modules with `vi.mock`, because the root Vitest config has no `@/` alias; Biome warns about it (a warning, not an error).
  - **Phone agent list, search and peek: the last message showed citations and raw Markdown.** The phone's agent row stripped Markdown from the agent's preview but did not run `cleanAgentMessageText` (the desktop's `cleanPreview` does), so a Codex reply showed `:codex-file-citation{path=…}` and private-use citation marks. The search result for an agent showed the preview with no stripping at all (`**`, links, `$$`), and the long-press peek showed the citations too. Fix: `agentPreviewLine` (`apps/mobile/src/features/agents/model/agent-preview.ts`) = `markdownPreviewText(cleanAgentMessageText(preview))` for the list row and the search row; the peek cleans the citations before it renders the Markdown. Test: `apps/mobile/src/features/agents/model/agent-preview.test.ts` (failed with the old line, passes now).
  - **Dynamic Island and the chat's waiting bar: an Arabic or Hebrew preview ran left to right.** The island's reply and question text, its working rows, the agent name in the compact pill and the waiting bar's reply preview had no `dir`, so an RTL reply was laid out in the window's LTR direction: aligned left, its full stop on the wrong side, and in the one-line waiting bar the ellipsis cut the start of the sentence. The sidebar rows and search results already use `dir="auto"`. Fix: `dir="auto"` on `DynamicIslandIdentity`'s name and description (`packages/ui/src/dynamic-island-identity.tsx`), the working row's name and task and `CompactAgentName` (`OpenBotDynamicIsland.tsx`), and `.awaiting-replies-preview` (`AwaitingReplies.tsx`). Test: `src/renderer/src/features/dynamic-island/fork-preview-direction.test.tsx` (3 tests, all failed before, all pass now).
  - **Workflow notifications showed the step's raw Markdown on several lines.** A workflow's "output" or "approval" notification (macOS) used the step's text as written, cut at 200 UTF-16 units: `## Digest`, `**Three**`, `- [Crash on start](https://…)`, each on its own line. Cause: `forwardWorkflowNotice` in `src/main/renderer-forwarders.ts` only redacted and trimmed the text. Fix: it uses `plainPreview` (the agent notifications' summary: whole sentences, Markdown stripped, one line, cut at whole characters). Test: `src/main/fork-workflow-notice.test.ts` (failed before, passes now; `electron` is mocked, which the repository's rule allows).
  - **Previews showed math as TeX source, and the Live Activity garbled matrices.** Every one-line preview (`markdownPreviewText`: sidebar, notifications, Dynamic Island, search, "Needs you", channel list, phone list) showed a formula as its TeX: `The answer is \frac{1}{2}.`, `\begin{pmatrix}1&2\\3&4\end{pmatrix}`. The iPhone Live Activity converts TeX to Unicode (`texToUnicode` in `live-activity-text.ts`), but it turned a matrix into `beginpmatrix1&2 3&4endpmatrix`, `\vec{v}\cdot\hat{x}` into `vecv·hatx`, `\|v\|` into `\\|v\\|`, and dropped `\{ \}`. Fix: the converter moves to `packages/contracts/src/tex-preview-text.ts` (`texPreviewText`) and learns environments (`pmatrix` → `(1 2; 3 4)`, `bmatrix` → `[…]`, `vmatrix`, `Vmatrix`, `cases`, `aligned`, `array`), accents on one letter (`v⃗`, `x̂`, `x̄`, `ẋ`, `x̃`), `\|` → `‖`, kept `\{ \}`, and a command next to a converted group no longer glues to it (`\cdot\hat{x}` → `·x̂`). `markdownPreviewText` uses it for math; `markdownPlainText` (copy and the phone's text selection) keeps the TeX. Test: `src/renderer/src/fork-preview-math.test.ts` (9 tests; 8 failed before, all pass now); the Dynamic Island test now expects `x²`.

## 101. Chat surfaces bug hunt: question cards, file chips and the file panel

- **Get:** question cards (`ask_ui`), file cards and the side-panel file view show their text as written: math typeset in every card field, names that cannot spoof, and previews that read the file. One line per fix below.
- **Why:** Elias, 24 Nov 2026, after #98: "Bug hunt … rendering on the chat surfaces outside the main Markdown core". Found with edge-case cards and files in Storybook (headless Chromium) and jsdom.
- **Fixes:**
  - **Math in every plain card field.** The `ask_ui` description tells agents "write every field as plain text; write math as LaTeX in $…$, which the app typesets", but only the title, option labels, form labels and segmented options went through `UiMathText`. A choice option's description (`meta`), a confirm field's label and value, the answer rows of an answered form with a secret field (label and answer), the answered outcome line, and a media caption showed raw `$F(1) = \frac{1}{3}$`. Now they go through `UiMathText` too (`UiChoiceBlock`, `UiConfirmBlock`, `UiFormBlock` `FormAnswerRows`, `UiBlockCard` outcome and badge, `UiBlockMedia` caption). Test: `src/renderer/src/features/conversation/UiBlockMath.test.tsx` › "math in the plain text fields of a card" (5 tests: 5 failed before, 5 pass after). Story `Conversation/UiBlocks` › `MathInEveryField`. Shots: `~/OpenBot/Shared/gotham/chat-bughunt/surfaces/card-math-before.png`, `card-math-after.png`.
  - **`\$` in card text shows a plain `$`.** `UiMathText` (card titles, labels, fields) kept the backslash of an escaped dollar, so "It costs \$5, not \$10." showed `\$5`, while hints and the chat (Markdown) show `$5`. An agent escapes a dollar so that it is not math. Now an odd run of backslashes before `$` shows one `$` and does not open math; `\\$x$` still keeps its backslashes and typesets `x`. Other backslashes stay as written. Test: `UiBlockMath.test.tsx` › "an escaped dollar in card text" (3 tests: 2 failed before, 3 pass after). Shots: `dollar-escape-before.png`, `dollar-escape-after.png`.
  - **Long labels and line breaks in card rows.** The answer rows of an answered form with a secret field had `dt { flex: none }`: a long label did not wrap, ran out of the card and squeezed the answer to one word per line (or out of sight). A textarea answer and a confirm field value lost their line breaks ("Flat 41, 7th floor Main Street 1 1000 Brussels"). Now the label wraps (`flex: 0 1 auto`), the answer keeps at least `min(192px, 50%)`, and `.ui-block-answer-value` and `.ui-block-fields dd` keep line breaks (`white-space: pre-wrap`), in `styles/ui-blocks.css`. Layout cannot be checked in jsdom, so the check is a headless Chromium script on the story: `~/OpenBot/Shared/gotham/chat-bughunt/surfaces/answer-rows-check.mjs` (FAIL before: row overflows by 44 px, answer outside the card, confirm value line breaks lost; PASS after). Story `Conversation/UiBlocks` › `LongAndMultilineFields`. Shots: `answer-rows-before.png`, `answer-rows-after.png`.
  - **A file name cannot spoof its extension.** A name with a right-to-left override (U+202E), such as `invoice<U+202E>fdp.command`, showed as `invoicednammoc.pdf` on the file card, in the message box, in a file chip inside a message and in the file panel title, but "Open" runs a `.command` file. A teammate or an agent on a joined server can send such a file. Now `visibleFileName` (`packages/ui/src/features/conversation/FileReference.ts`) removes the embedding, override and isolate controls (U+202A–U+202E, U+2066–U+2069) from the shown name and its labels in `AttachmentCards`, `ComposerAttachmentRow` (files and uploads), `RichMessageText` file chips and the `FilePreviewPanel` title. The file and its real name are not changed. Test: `src/renderer/src/features/conversation/FileNameBidi.test.tsx` (4 tests: 4 failed before, 4 pass after). Story `Conversation/AttachmentCards` › `UnusualNames` (also `$`, `&`, `#`, `|`, emoji and Hebrew names, which showed correctly). Shots: `file-name-bidi-before.png`, `file-name-bidi-after.png` (cards and panel title).
  - **UTF-16 text files read right in the file panel.** The panel decoded every text and Markdown file as UTF-8, so a file with a UTF-16 byte order mark (PowerShell `>` output, Excel "Unicode text", Notepad "Unicode") showed `��Name;Price`, `Caf�` and hidden NUL characters, and Copy copied them. Now `decodedFileText` (`packages/ui/src/features/conversation/file-text.ts`) reads the byte order mark: `FF FE` UTF-16LE, `FE FF` UTF-16BE, else UTF-8 as before. One line changes in `FilePreviewPanel.tsx` (the `source` memo). Test: `src/renderer/src/features/conversation/FilePreviewText.test.tsx` (3 tests: the 2 UTF-16 tests failed before, 3 pass after). Shots: `utf16-text-before.png`, `utf16-text-after.png`.
  - **A `.tsv` file shows as text in the file panel.** `attachmentMimeTypeForName` had no type for `.tsv`, so it was `application/octet-stream` and the panel said "Preview unavailable" while a `.csv` showed as text. Now `.tsv` is `text/tab-separated-values`, so `filePreviewKindForFile` gives `text` (`packages/contracts/src/attachment-files.ts`). Which files can be attached does not change (`.tsv` is still not in the attach list). Test: `src/renderer/src/features/conversation/file-preview-kind.test.ts` (2 tests: the `.tsv` test failed before, 2 pass after). Shots: `tsv-preview-before.png`, `tsv-preview-after.png`.
  - **YAML front matter in a `.md` file shows as YAML.** A Markdown file that starts with front matter (a `SKILL.md`, an Obsidian note) showed a rule and then all its keys as one big bold heading ("name: pdf-tools description: …"), because `---` / `key: value` / `---` is a thematic break plus a setext heading in Markdown. Now `MarkdownFilePreview` shows a leading `---` block whose first line is a `key:` line as a fenced `yaml` code block (the fence is longer than any backtick run inside). A file that starts with a rule and plain text is unchanged; the raw view (`<>`) and Copy keep the file as written. Test: `FilePreviewText.test.tsx` › "a Markdown file with YAML front matter" (2 tests: 1 failed before, 2 pass after). Shots: `md-front-matter-before.png`, `md-front-matter-after.png`.
